ētil piṇantaḻīi yaṟṟu.
poruṭporuḷār puṉṉalan tōyār aruṭporuḷ
āyum aṟivi ṉavar.
potunalattār puṉṉalan tōyār matinalattiṉ
māṇṭa aṟivi ṉavar.
tannalam pārippār tōyār takicerukkip
puṉṉalam pārippār tōḷ.
niṟineñca millavar tōyvar piṟaneñciṟ
pēṇip puṇarpavar tōḷ.
āyu maṟiviṉa rallārk kaṇaṅkeṉpa
māya makaḷir muyakku.
varivilā māṇiḻiyār meṉṟōḷ puriyilāp
pūriyarka ḷāḻum aḷaṟu.
irumaṉap peṇṭiruṅ kaḷḷuṅ kavaṟun
tirunīkkap paṭṭār toṭarpu.
cilar purāṇak katikaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvar. ivar oḻukkattil nāṭṭamuṭiyavarākār. cila tattuva nuṭpaṅkaḷip peṇṇāka uruvakap paṭuttik kavikaḷ pāṭiyatip poruḷāk koḷvatu maṭami. avaṟṟik kāṭṭi ulaki ēmāṟṟap pukuvatu kayami. oruvaṉ oruttiyuṭaṉ vāḻtalē aṟam; aṟam. pala peṇkaḷi oruvaṉ maṇakkalām eṉṉuṅ kaṭṭukkatikaḷ iṉi otukkappaṭal vēṇṭum.
vipacārak kūṭṭatti oḻuṅkupaṭuttal
piṟappāl patiyilār eṉṟu collappaṭuṅ kūṭṭattiṉ kuṟipāṭukaḷi niṟivu ceytal aritaṉṟu. ulakil potuvāka vuḷḷa patiyilā vipacārak kūṭṭattic cīrceyvatu mika aritu. ikkūṭṭam perukutaṟkuk kāraṇam vaṟumiyāyiruttalāl, aṟavōr āṅkāṅkē viṭutic cālikaḷ amittu, appeṇ maṇikaṭkuc cōṟu kūṟi utavi, kalvi kaṟpittu, kittoḻil pōtittu, virumpuvōrkku maṇañ ceytu vippatu nalam. nāṭṭil celvar palar illāmalilli. aṉṉār maṉam it tuṟiyil tirumputal vēṇṭum. ēḻip peṇmakkaṭkeṉa ōr aṟaccāli amippatu ettuṇiyō vēṟu pala aṟaccāli kaḷ niṟuviya payaṉi nalkum. celvar tamatu celvatti ivvaṟat tuṟiyil payaṉpaṭuttuvārāka. aracu, celvattip potumi yākkiṉ vaṟumip pēyē māṇṭu pōkum.
celvarkaḷāl peṇṇulakaṅ keṭal
ulakil celvarē pala kēṭṭiṟkuṅ kāraṇarāyiruttal kaṇkūṭu. ēḻittoḻilāḷar uḻippāl peṟuñ celvatti mutalāḷikaḷ palatiṟak kaḷiyāṭalkaḷukkuc celavu ceytal peruntavaṟu. kaḷiyāṭalkaḷuḷ peṇmakkaḷik kaṟpaḻikkum āṭal taliyāyatu. celvarkkup peṇ vaṭivaṅkaḷ eṉṉa vākat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉavō teriyavilli. peṇ oru kaḷip poruḷ eṉpatu aṉṉār karuttup pōlum!
celvar poruṭṭup patiyilāp peṇmakkaḷ taṅkaḷ niliyi maṟantu, taṅkaḷi oru vilip poruḷākak karuti vāḻkiṟārkaḷ. celvar koṭumiyāl peṇmakkaḷiṉ tey vikamum karantu niṟkiṟatu. teyvap peṇkaḷip pēyākkuñ celvar āṇṭavaṉi uṇarum nāḷum uṇṭō? peṇṇit teyvamākap pōṟṟum oru nili peṟuvōṉ kaṭavuḷik kāṇum aṉpaṉāvaṉ; aruḷāḷaṉāvaṉ.
ulakil patiyilār kūṭṭam eṉṟoṉṟu illātavāṟu oḻikka āṅkāṅkē aṉpum aṟivum vāynta periyōr muyalvārāka. avar muyaṟcikku iyaṟkiyum iṟiyum tuṇiceyyum. iyaṟki iṟiyiṉ nōkkam peṇṇulakil patiyilār iruttal vēṇṭumeṉpataṉṟu. ceyaṟki oḻiya, iyaṟki iṟiyum tuṇipuriyum. ulakil patiyilāk kūṭṭam oḻika; oḻika. peṇṇulakil kaṟi aḻika; aḻika. peṇṇulaku ōṅka; ōṅka.
aṉpiṉ viḻiyār poruḷviḻiyum āytoṭiyār
iṉco liḻukkut tarum 	-tiruvaḷḷuvar


12. kimmi
(kimpeṇ niliyum ciṟumikaḷum - kuḻanti maṇak koṭumi - aiṉa kaṇitak kuṟippu - intu matattiṉ poṟuppu - maṟumaṇam - cāttiram; karmam - kimpeṇkaḷiṉ tuyar - kaḷiyum vaḻi).
tirumaṇañ ceytu koṇṭa orutti, taṉ koḻunaṉip paṟṟiya oru ceytiyum peṟātoḻiyiṉum allatu avaḷ talivaṉ koliyuṇṭā ṉāyiṉum allatu iṟantu paṭṭāṉāyiṉum avaḷ kaṉṉiyā yiruppā ḷāyiṉ, avaḷukku maṟumaṇañ ceytu koḷḷum urimi uṇṭu. 	-pōtāyaṉar.
nāṭṭuk kimpeṇ nili
kimmi eṉṉun talippiṭumpōtē yāṉ piṟanta nāṭṭiṉmītu nāṭṭañ celkiṟatu. kimmi nili peṇṇula kiṟkum āṇulakiṟkum potuvāṉatu. annili vāḻvil irupālāruḷ evarkkēṉum oruvarkku uṟāmaṟ pōkātu. ulakil pala iṭaṅkaḷil āṇkimmiyarip pōlavē peṇkimmiyarum vāḻntu varukiṉṟaṉar. cila iṭaṅkaḷil ciṟappāka namatu nāṭṭil kimmi eytum peṇmakkaṭkup palatiṟac ciṟumikaḷ uṇṭu. namatu nāṭṭiṟ potuvākap peṇṇulakiṟkē ciṟumi atikam. atilum kimpeṇkaḷuk kuḷḷa ciṟumikaḷ collaṟpālaṉavalla. m¢áWikfŸ ÃidÉYW« nghJ v‹ò be¡F ÉL»wJ; cŸs§FiH »wJ ‘ï¥ghG« eh£oš V‹ ãwªnjh«? eṉṟu niṉintu niṉintu iraṅkutalum nērkiṟatu.
peṇmakaḷ kimmi eytiṉāl eṉṉa? āṇ makaṉ annili eytuvatilliyō? peṇmakaḷ kimmi eytiya tum avaḷpāluḷḷa iyaṟki ōṭiviṭukiṟatō? uyir pōy viṭukiṟatō? kaṭavuḷ maṟintu viṭukiṟārō? paṭippil eṉṉa kuṟivu ēṟpaṭukiṟatu? avaḷ etiril varakkūṭātām! kūṭṭaṅ kaḷil pōkak kūṭātām! nalviṉikaḷil talikāṭṭak kūṭātām! uṭiyiyum uṇaviyum curukkal vēṇṭumām! appeṇ mūtēviyāy viṭṭāḷām! kimmi eytiya āṇmakaṉō ellāvaṟṟiṉuṅ kalantu koḷḷalām! avaṉ palliḻanta mutiyaṉāyiṉum patiṉāṟu vayatu kaṭṭaḻakiyi maṇañ ceyyalām! eṉṉa vēṟṟumi! ivvēṟṟumi iyaṟkiyil uḷḷatā? kaṭavuḷ kaṇṭatā? iḵtu irakkamilā vaṉkaṇāḷar iṭik kālattil vititta koṭumi! koṭumi!
kuḻanti maṇak koṭumi
nam nāṭṭavariṉ aniyāya meṉṉa? 1ciṟukuḻanti - ōrāṇṭuk kuḻanti - ataṟku maṇameṉṉa teriyum? pacciḷaṅ kuḻavikkup peṇ āṇpāl vēṟṟumiyātal teriyumā? ak kuḻantikkut tirumaṇam! atu kimmi eytiṉāl ataṟkuṅ kaṭṭuppāṭu! akkuḻavi ulaki aṟintatā? illiyē! atu tāymi eṉṉum cīriya niliyi aṭiyāmalaṉṟō oḻikiṟatu! itu kaṭavuḷ karuttākumā? namatu nāṭṭil ettuṇip peṇ makkaḷ pālmaṇamaṟā iḷaṅkuḻantip paruvattil kimmi eyti varuntukiṟārkaḷ!
jaṉa kaṇitam
1921-ām āṇṭiṉ jaṉak kaṇakki yoṭṭik kimmi eytiya peṇmakkaḷ mītu ciṟitu karuttuc celuttuvōm. 
appuḷḷikaḷi uṟṟu nōkkuḻip potuvāka intiyap peṇmaṇikaḷiṉ niliyum, ciṟappāka hintu peṇmaṇikaḷiṉ niliyum iṉitu pulaṉākum. ikkāla hintu matattip peṇkoli matam eṉṟu kūṟal mikiyākātu. intiyā viluḷḷa maṟṟak kimpeṇkaḷiṉ tokiyilum, hintu kimpeṇkaḷ tokiyē mēmpaṭṭiruttal karutaṟpāṟṟu. intiyāviṉ mottak kimpeṇ toki 26,834,838. hintu kimpeṇ toki 20,218,780. eñciyuḷḷa toki mikac ciṟiyatu. atuvum mulimkaḷ - kiṟituvar - puttar - mutaliyōriluḷḷa kimpeṇkaḷ aṭaṅkiya toki.
oruvayatu mutal patiṉintu vayatu vari, kimmi eyti yuḷḷa intiyap peṇkuḻantikaḷ toki 396,556. itil hintu peṇkuḻantikaḷ toki 329,076. ivvaniyāyatti uṟṟu nōkkuṅkaḷ! ivvaḷavu pālmaṇamaṟā iḷaṅkim peṇkaḷ! eṉṉē! ikkolikkup poṟuppu yātu? hintu matamaṉṟō? maṟṟa mataṅkaḷiluḷḷavar mīṇṭun tirumaṇañ ceyyum urimi peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. mēlum avar jaṉa kaṇitam eṭutta vēḷiyil kimmi eytiyiruntavar. avaruḷ et tuṇiyō pēr piṉṉi maṇañ ceytiruppar. hintu kuḻantikaḷiṉ niliyō! niliyō!.... eṉṟu aḻavēṇṭuvatē!
namatu nāṭṭil ilaṭcakkaṇakkāṉa ciṟukuḻantikaḷ kimpeṇkaḷāyirukkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kati eṉṉi? tirumaṇam iṉṉateṉ ṟuṇarāmuṉṉar ēṉ ciṟumiyarit tirumaṇañ ceytu koṭuttal vēṇṭum? kimmi eyta nērntāl avari ēṉ kūṭṭil aṭittu varuttal vēṇṭum? avar vaḻukki vīḻvarēl avari aṭippatu! ūriviṭṭu ōṭṭuvatu! camayam nērntāl kolvatu! eṉṉa pāvam! ip pāvaṅkaḷi nikaḻttivarum nāṭṭukku uyvuṇṭō? urimi yuṇṭō? nāyakaṉuṭaṉ orupoḻutēṉum kūṭi iṉpam nukarāta peṇ makaḷ kimminili eytiṉāl, mīṇṭum maṇañ ceytalā kātā? maṇam eṉpatu yātu? pantarā? kōyilā? mantiramā? māliyā? orutti oruvaṉuṭaṉ kūṭi iṉpam nukaru nāḷē tirumaṇam muṭinta nāḷākum. iḷami maṇattiyum vilakkātu, maṟumaṇattiyum taṭuppatu aṟamākātu.
maṟumaṇap poṟuppu
uṟṟa vayataṭintu, pala āṇṭu, talivaṉuṭaṉ kūṭik kulāvip piḷḷip pēṟu peṟṟa orutti, kimpeṇ āvaḷēl, avaḷ maṟumaṇañ ceyyāma lirukkalām. āṉāl avaṭkum taṉ viruppappaṭi naṭakkum urimi iruttal vēṇṭum. avaḷ virumpiṉāl maṟumaṇañ ceytu koḷḷalām. avaḷ vipacāram ceytu keṭṭu aḻivatiṉum, oruvaṉi maṇantu avaṉuṭaṉ vāḻtal ciṟappākum. maṇam virumpuṅ kim peṇṇit taṭuttaluṅ kūṭātu; virumpātavaḷi valiyuṟut taluṅkūṭātu. poṟuppup peṇṇiṉuṭiyatā yiruttal vēṇṭum.
cila āṇṭu nāyakaṉuṭaṉ vāḻntu, iṉpam nukarntu, piḷḷip pēṟu peṟṟuk, kimmi eytuvōḷ mīṇṭum maṟṟoruvaṉi naccavēṇṭuvatilli. appeṇmaṇi vēṟu pala aṟattuṟikaḷil iṟaṅki vāḻvu naṭāttal nalaṉ.
cāttiram karmam
pālmaṇamaṟā iḷaṅ kimpeṇkaḷ uṟṟa vayataṭintu maṟumaṇañ ceytu koḷvatu aṟamē yākum. ataṟku aṟiñar tuṇi ceytē tīral vēṇṭum. cāttiram - karmam - ivimītu vīṇpaḻi cumattuvatu aṟiyāmi.
cāttiram etu? iyaṟki neṟikku māṟupaṭṭu eḻutap paṭṭaṉa vellāñ cāttiraṅkaḷ ākumā? ākā! cāttiram eṉpataṉ poruḷ eṉṉi? itic cey; itic ceyyaṟka eṉpatu. iyaṟkikku uriyatic ceytal vēṇṭum; iyaṟkikku māṟupaṭṭatic ceytalākātu. iti maṉaccāṉṟu avvap pōtu uṇarttivarum. ituvē cāttiram. tiṉṟu koḻuttup piṟari aṭakkiyāḷa, iyaṟkikku muṟṟum araṇ ceyyāta poruḷ potiya, muraṭar cilar eḻutiya ēṭukaḷ yāvuñ cāttiraṅkaḷākā. iyaṟkiyē periya cāttiram. ataṉ niyatikaḷ, vitikaḷ. avvitikaḷ vaḻi, maṉpati naṭāttal vēṇṭum. 
kimmi eytiya peṇ, mīṇṭum maṇam ceyyalākā teṉṟu iyaṟkiyil ētāyiṉum niyati uṇṭā? peṇ kimmi eytiyatum avaḷ pāluḷḷa iyaṟki vēṭki avaḷi viṭuttu, nīttu ōṭukiṟatā? cāttiram cāttiram eṉṟu iruṭṭaṟiyil vipacārañ ceytu karuccitikkalām pōlum! iṅkē,kaṭavuḷ pāvam karmam oṉṟumilli pōlum! veḷippaṭiyāka oruvaṉi maṇañ ceyvatu maṭṭum pāvam pōlum! eṉṉa aṟiyāmi! iyaṟkikku māṟupaṭṭa kaṇmūṭi vaḻakkamum kōḻamiyum nāttikattiṉpāṟ paṭṭaṉavā meṉka.
karmattiṉ mītu ciṟitu karuttuc celuttuvōm. karmam uṇṭu; illi eṉṟu collavilli. teruvē pōkum oru piḷḷiyiṉ kāl, vaṇṭiyil akappaṭṭu nacuṅkiviṭukiṟatu. atu kaṇṭu, karmam eṉṟu vāḷā kiṭappatu aṟivuṭimi yākumō? ulakam aṅṅaṉam vāḷā kiṭakkiṟatō? uṭaṉē ak kālukkut takka cikicci ceyyavē makkaḷ puṟappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. aṟiyāmiyāl karmam iyalpāka nikaḻum. ataṟkuk kaḻuvāyum iyaṟkiyilirukkiṟatu. kuḻanti, karmattiṉpaṭi kimmi eytiyirukkalām. ataṟkuk kaḻuvāy eṉṉi? uṟṟa vayatil atu taṉ iyaṟki vēṭkiyit taṇikka maṇañ ceytu koḷvatākum.
karmattiṉ pērālum kaṭavuḷ pērālum namatu nāṭṭil pala koṭumikaḷ nikaḻttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu iḷam kimpeṇkaḷit tuṉpuṟuttuvatu. attuṉpam nāṭṭi viṭuttu akalal vēṇṭum. iḷaṅ kimpeṇkaḷ tuyar kaṇṭu maṉam iraṅkātavar ilar. avartam tuyar kaḷital peruṅkaṭaṉ eṉṟu niṉippōrumuḷar. āṉāl niṉivic ceyalil koṇara avartam maṉam eḻuvatilli. kāraṇam eṉṉi? nīṇṭanāḷ vaḻakka oḻukkap pēykaḷum, akkam pakkattuk kūkkural - cātip paḻakkam mutaliya pūtaṅkaḷum kuṟuk kiṭutalēyākum. ippēy pūtaṅkaṭkā aṭimiyāvatu? maṉac cāṉṟeṉuṅ kaṭavuḷ vaḻiyaṉṟō niṟṟal aṟivu? akkaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭiṟku uṇmiyil uṟutiyum añcā neñcamum tēvi.
kimpeṇkaḷiṉ tuyar
kimpeṇkaḷ vīṭukaḷiṟ paṭuntuyarukku ōr aḷavum uṇṭō? cila vēḷikaḷil avarkaḷi īṉṟa tāy tanti yarum kāykiṉṟaṉar; aṇṇaṉ tampimār veṟukkiṉṟaṉar; maṟṟavarum kuṟi kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. kāyvum veṟuppum poṟātu ettuṇit tāymār taṟkoli ceytukoḷkiṟār; ettuṇic cakōtarimār vīṭṭi viṭuttu ōṭi alikiṟār; ettuṇip pēr evar utaviyum nāṭātu ili tittum, paḻam viṟṟum, appañ cuṭṭum, kūlivēli ceytum kāl vayiṟuṇṭu maraṇatti etirnōkki vāḻkiṟār. namatu nāṭṭuk kimpeṇkaḷ tuyari eṉṉeṉṟu colvēṉ! evveḻut tāl eḻutuvēṉ! innūlil atupaṟṟi virittuk kūṟalum anāvaciyam.
kimpeṇkaḷ tuyar kaḷiya nāṭṭiṟ piṟanta ovvoru varkkum ūkkam eḻuvatāka. ataṟkuriya muṟikaḷ pala kūṟalām. cila varumāṟu:
(1) 	cāttirak kuppikaḷik kaṭṭi aḻalākātu; iyaṟkic cāttiravaḻi niṟkap payilal vēṇṭum.
(2) 	iḷami maṇam niṟuttappaṭal vēṇṭum.
(3) 	nāyakaṉuṭaṉ orupoḻutēṉum kūṭi vāḻntu iṉpam nukarātu, kimmi eytiya peṇ makkaḷ tirumaṇam puriyattakka vaḻiyil vāḻvu muṟikaḷic cīrtiruttañ ceytal vēṇṭum.
(4) 	nāyakaṉuṭaṉ kūṭik kulāviya piṉṉik kimmi eytuvōrkkup palatiṟa aṟaccāli kalviccāli toḻiṟ cālikaḷ amikkappaṭal vēṇṭum.
iḷaṅ kimpeṇkaḷiṉ ciṟumiyāl, peṇṇulakiṉ perumikkuriya tāymikkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum peruṅ kēṭu nikaḻkiṟatu. oṉṟum aṟiyāta vayatil tirumaṇameṉṉum peyarāl veṟumaṇam peṟṟup piṉṉik kimpeṇṇāyk kiṭappatā peṇpiṟaviyiṉ nōkkam? peṇ aṭiyattakka perum pēṟṟukkuk kimminili peruntaṭiyākavum niṟkiṟatu. kimpeṇkaḷāl āṇulakiṟkum pala tiṟak kēṭukaḷ uṇṭu. ātalāl kimmip peṇkaḷum peṇṇulakil cērntavarkaḷākalāṉ, avarkaḷum taṅkaḷiṉattavarukkuriya viḻuppam peṟutaṟku evarum iṭiyūṟu ceyvōrāka niṟṟalākātu; iṭiyūṟāka niṟṟal tāykkoli ceyta pāvamāka muṭiyum. kimpeṇkaḷiṉ ciṟumi eṉṉum nōy nīkkam, peṇṇulakiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattukkup perum vaḻi tiṟakkum. peṇṇulakiluḷḷa akkuṟi nīṅka, ellām valla āṇṭavaṉ nāṭṭavarkku nallaṟivu koḷuttuvāṉāka.
tirumaṇañ ceytu kaṉṉi vāḻvu naṭāttum peṇ taṉ kaṇavaṉi iḻakkap peṟuvaḷēl, mīṇṭum maṟumaṇam purintu koḷḷalām.
vākkuṟuti koṭutta piṉṉarō - nīrvārttut tattañ ceyta piṉṉarō - meyyuṟal nikaḻā muṉṉarō - koḻunaṉ ivvulaka vāḻvu nīppaṉēl, peṇ maṟumaṇañ ceytu koḷḷalām.
uṟuti moḻiyum mantira moḻiyum oruttiyiṉ piramacariya viratattik kulippaṉavākā. avaḷ maṟṟavaṉukkuk koṭukkappaṭalām.	-vaciṣṭar
“Hinduism is the only religion which presents the sad anomalies of thousands of child widows”	-Ganga Ram


13. peṇmi - tāymi  - iṟimi
(peṇmi - tāymi - iṟimi - aṉpē iṟi - tāymiyil iṟimi ilaṅkum vitam - peṇ teyvam - peṇṇiṉ iṟimi - srīrāmapirāṉ muyaṟci - pāratap pōr - ellām peṇṇāl - ellām peṇṇil.)
cemminalam aṟiyāta citaṭaroṭun tirivēṉi
mummimalam aṟuvittu mutalāya mutalvaṉṟāṉ
nammiyumōr poruḷākki nāyciviki ēṟṟuvitta
ammiyeṉak karuḷiyavā ṟārpeṟuvā raccōvē
	-vātavūraṭikaḷ
peṇmi  eṉpatu anātiyāka muḻu mutaṟporuḷiṉ orukūṟāyiruppatu. appeṇmi taṇmic cattiyāyt taṉak kuriya kaṭaṉi āṟṟi varukiṟatu. accattiyiṉ kūṟē peṇṇula keṉpatu. peṇ, makaḷākap piṟantu, piṉṉi maṉiviyāki, piṉṉit tāyāki, muṭivil iṟiyākiṟāḷ. peṇṇi ṉiṭattu ittuṇi nilikaḷ iruttal innūṟkaṇ viḷakkap paṭṭirukkiṟatu.
peṇmi
peṇmi eṉpatu kaṭpulaṉāyatōr amitittaṉmi. amiti niliyē ciṟanta kaṭavuḷnili eṉṟu maṟi muḻaṅkukiṟatu. kaṭavuḷi yāṇṭum cāntam cāntam eṉṟu vētāntam pōṟṟukiṟatu. amiti, peṇ vaṭivākak kaṇṇukkuk kāṭci aḷikkiṟatu; kāṭci aḷippatōṭu nillātu, ulaka vāḻvil talippaṭṭuk kaṭavuḷ neṟiyik kāṭṭukiṟatu; anneṟiyāl iṟimik kāṭciyiyum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. peṇmikkaṇuḷḷa innuṭpaṅkaḷi yellām ulakam uṇarvatāka.
tāymi
peṇmikkuḷḷa perumi yātu? peṇmiyiṉ māṭṭu, ulaka vaḷarccik kuriyatum toṇṭuk kuriyatumākiya tāymi politalāṉ, atu perumiyuṭiyatākiṟatu. peṇmik kuḷḷa perumi yellām tāymiyāl eṉka.
tāymiyil nilavuvatu iṟimi. iṟimi peṇmi yiṉ muṭintaniliyākum.
iṟimi
iṟi etu? camaya nūlkaḷ palavāṟu kūṟum. akkūṟṟuk kaḷi īṇṭu ārāya vēṇṭuvatilli. poy, poṟāmi, avā, cīṟṟam, taṉṉalam mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṭanta oṉṟu iṟi eṉpatu. oruvar uḷḷattil aṉpu nikaḻum pōtu ippoy, poṟāmi mutaliyaṉa nilavumō? poy, poṟāmi, avā mutaliyavaṟṟāl eriyum oruvaṉ uḷḷattil aṉpu otuṅki niṟṟal, (ovvoruvar tam tam) anupavattāl uṇarak kūṭiyatu.
aṉpu
aṉpu eṉpatu poy poṟāmi mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṭantu niṟpatu. poy, poṟāmi mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṭantu niṟkum oṉṟē iṟi eṉṟuñ collappaṭukiṟatu. ākavē iṟiyē aṉpu, aṉpē iṟiyātal kāṇka.
aṉpuñ civamum iraṇṭeṉpar aṟivilār
aṉpē civamāva tārum aṟikilār
aṉpē civamāva tārum aṟintapiṉ
aṉpē civamā yamarntirun tārē 	- tirumūlar
tāymiyil iṟimi ilaṅkum vitam
peṇ, piḷḷi peṟṟatun tāyākiṟāḷ. tāy piḷḷiyi vaḷarkkap pukuṅkāl, avaḷ uḷḷattil iṟi mikkuriya nīrmikaḷellām patikiṉṟaṉa. toṇṭu, tiyākam, taṉṉala maṟuppu mutaliyaṉa avaḷ māṭṭu arumpukiṉṟaṉa. tāy kim māṟu karutik kuḻantikkut toṇṭu ceyvatilli. taṉakkuḷḷa ellāvaṟṟiyum - camayam nēriṉ uyiriyum - piḷḷi nalattukkuk koṭukkat tāy virintu niṟkiṟāḷ. taṉṉalaṅ karutik kuḻantiyi varuttun tāy yāṇṭum irāḷ. payaṉ karutāt toṇṭu, tiyākam, taṉṉala maṟuppu mutaliyaṉa cērnta oṉṟē tāymi eṉka. innīrmikaḷ uḷḷa iṭattil poṟāmi avā mutaliyaṉa irumpuṇṭa nīrpōla oṭuṅkip pōkum. innilipeṟṟa tāyiṉ uḷḷattil eṉṉa nilavum? aṉpākiya iṟiyaṉṟō nilavum? tāyuḷḷattil aṉpē ūrntiruttalāl, aṉpukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭākat tāyaṉpik koḷvatu āṉṟōr vaḻakkam. tāy eṉiṉum aṉpeṉiṉum okkum.
iṉṉum iraṇṭoru piḷḷikaḷip peṟun tāyaṉpu colaṟpālataṉṟu. avvaṉpu kūṭap peṟṟa tāy, maṟṟap piḷḷikaḷiyum taṉ piḷḷikaḷippōṟ karutum orunili eytukiṟāḷ. annili evvuyiriyum taṉṉuyirpōl pārkkum perunili kūṭṭum. apperunilikkut tāymi nilik kaḷāṉā  yirukkiṟatu. poy poṟāmi kaṭanta aṉpē, tāymi yākaliṉ, tāymi iṟimiyākiṟatu.
aṉpukkaṭavuḷ kōyil
poy poṟāmikaḷit tavirkkum toṇṭu, tiyākam, taṉṉala miṉmi mutaliyaṉa paṭiyum uḷḷamē aṉpuk kaṭavuḷuk kuriya kōyil. aṉpuk kaṭavuḷ, nilipēṟākat tāyākiya peṇ makaḷiṭattil oḷirkiṟatu. peṇmiyil tāymiyum, tāymi yil iṟimiyum polivatu karutattakkatu.
peṇ teyvam
peṇmi eṉpatu amitit taṉmi eṉṟu mēlē collappaṭṭatu. amitinili iṟimi eṉpatu. iṟimi yuṭiya tāyi veṟum peṇṇeṉṟā kūṟuvatu? avaḷi peṇ teyvameṉṟu pōṟṟutal vēṇṭum. peṇ - teyvam! peṇ - teyvam! peṇ - teyvam!
muḻu mutaṟ poruḷ, taṇmi vemmi yeṉum iraṇṭu caktiyāka ulaki iyakkukiṟateṉpatu nūl toṭakkattil kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. at taṇmiyiṉ kūṟu peṇṇulaku. peṇ piṟantu, ulaka vaḷarccikkut tuṇipurintu, mīṇṭum at taṇmit teyvamākiṟāḷ.
peṇ, makaḷākat tōṉṟiṉāḷ; maṉiviyāka vāḻn tāḷ; tāyākat toṇṭu ceytāḷ. ippoḻutu teyva mākak kāṭci aḷikkiṟāḷ. ulakīr! akkāṭci kāṇmiṉ; teyvam teyvam eṉṟu eṅku ōṭukiṟīr? nūṟkaḷi ēṉ ārāy kiṟīr? itō teyvam; peṇ teyvam; kāṇuṅkaḷ; kaṇṭu vaḻipaṭuṅkaḷ.
peṇmi eṉpatu, eṉputōl pōrtta uṭalaṉṟu. avvuṭaliṉuḷḷē yuḷḷa nuṇmi,  iṟimi, peṇmi eṉka. attakiya peṇmi vāḻka.
āṇmakkaḷē! uṅkaḷ perumi peṇṇuṭaṉ porunti niṟpatu. uṅkaḷip peṟṟavaḷ peṇ; uṅkaḷi vaḷarppavaḷ peṇ; uṅkaḷōṭu vāḻpavaḷ peṇ; uṅkaḷip peṇṇākku pavaḷ peṇ. nīṅkaḷ peṇṇākātu, atāvatu peṇminili peṟātu, atāvatu amiti nili eytātu, viṭutaliyaṭital iyalātu. peṇṇāl nalaṉaṭiyum nīṅkaḷ appeṇṇu lakiyā veṟuppatu? tuṟappatu? naṉṟi kollātēyuṅkaḷ; tāynaṉṟi kollātēyuṅkaḷ; ennaṉṟi koṉṟārkkum uyvuṇṭām uyvilli- ceynnaṉṟi koṉṟa makaṟku eṉṉum poyyāmoḻiyi nōkkuṅkaḷ.
peṇṇiṟku matippuk koṭuṅkaḷ; urimi koṭuṅkaḷ; vaṇakkam celuttuṅkaḷ. peṇṇi matittup pōṟṟalē nākarikam. avaḷik kaṭṭuppaṭuttal - aṭimippaṭuttal - koṭumiyāka naṭattal - anākarikam.
peṇṇalaṉ uṅkaḷ nalaṉ; uṅkaḷ nāṭṭu nalaṉ. peṇ kaṇṇīrviṭum nāṭu vaṟumi nāṭākum; aṭimi nāṭākum. aḵtu erintu pōkum; karintu pōkum. oru peṇṇukku srīrāmapirāṉ evvaḷavu muyaṉṟār; ettuṇi iṭukkaṇuṟṟār; ettuṇippōr purintār! oru peṇṇiṉ poruṭṭup pāratap perumpōr naṭantatu! peṇṇiṉ tuyar tīrppatu āṇmakaṉ kaṭami. avaḷatu tuyar tīrkka iyalātavaṉ āṇ makaṉallaṉ. peṇṇiṉ ciṟumi pōkka uyiriyun tuṟakkalām. avaḷatu perumi kākkavē āṇmakaṉ piṟantirukkiṟāṉ. peṇṇilliyēl ulakētu? uyirētu? aṉpētu? kaṭavuḷētu? ellām peṇṇāl; ellām peṇṇil. attakiya peṇṇi - tāyi - teyvattip pōṟṟuṅkaḷ; pōṟṟuṅkaḷ.
eṅkaṇum peṇmi polika; eṅkaṇun tāymi ōṅka; eṅkaṇum iṟimi vāḻka. vantēmātaram.
oppuṭiya mātarum oṇporuḷum nī 	- appar
tāyē yāki vaḷarttaṉi pōṟṟi 	- māṇikkavācakar
tāymip peṇṇiṉam vāḻntu taḻikka
	tāymip peṇṇuyir tantiṭal kāṭci
tāymip peṇṇeṉac cāṉṟukaḷ vēṇṭā
	tāymip peṇṇiṉam veṉṟu taḻikka.	(1)
ulaka mellām utippiṭan tāymi
	utittut taṅki oḻukiṭan tāymi
kaliyiṉ mūlaṅ kamaḻiṭan tāymi
	kaṭavu ḷaṉpu curappiṭan tāymi.	(2)
vāṉi nōkka vayaṅkiṭun tāymi
	maṇṇi nōkka viḷaṅkiṭun tāymi
kāṉam māmali kāṭṭiṭum tāymi
	kaṭalum āṟum karuṇiyiṉ tāymi. 	(3)
karuṇip peṇṇik kaṭivatō āṇmi
	kaṭivō rentak karuvaḻi vantār?
aruḷi aṟṟavar ārtta moḻikaḷ
	aṉalik kakkum arunara kākum.	(4)
peṇṇip pēyeṉap pēcutal naṉṟō
	periya māyiyeṉ ṟēcutal naṉṟō
taṇmi yillā vaṉattu vilaṅkum
	taṭavip peṇṇalam tāṅkutal kāṇmiṉ.	(5)
pāvi tāli aṟuttiṭal mūrkkam
	paṭṭam muṇṭi eṉ ṟītalum mūrkkam
ētu maṟṟa iḻivukaḷ mūrkkam
	ellām vīḻtta iḷiñar eḻumiṉ.	(6)
āṇum peṇṇum camaṉcamaṉ aiyō
      āṇiṉ kōlmika aṉṉi viḻuntāḷ
kōṇal nīkkiṭuṅ kātal maṇamē
        kōtil mārkkam kulavum iṉitē	(8)

1	God is unknown and unknowable.	-Herbert  Spencer
1	yāṉ eḻutiyuḷḷa tirukkuṟaḷ virivuriyil poṟivāyil aintavittāṉ, aintavittāṉ āṟṟal mutaliya kuṟaṭpākkaṭku eṉṉāl poṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷi nōkkuka. pulaṉaṭakka nuṭpatti murukaṉ allatu aḻaku mutaliya nūlkaḷilum eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ.
1	iyalpuṭiya mūvar eṉpataṟkut tirukkuṟaḷ virivuriyil yāṉ eḻutiyuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷip pārkka.
1.	* * * They must understand that the life - giving fluid called the semen, which is produced in the creative organs of the man, is of great value in the upbuilding of his own body. It is only within comparatively recent times that the marvellous power of the creative fluid in building up and making over the body of the individual has been thoroughly understood. In sexual intimacies there is a discharge of this creative fluid from the body of the man, but where there is a full response on the part of the wife, there seems to be an exchange of magnetism or energy which makes up for the loss. If however, his desire alone is active and she is simply fulfilling a supposed wifely duty, she gives nothing to him, and he therefore, suffers a definite loss in vitality. It is climed by some that such one-sided intimacies are almost as harmful to the man as masturbation. Frequent indulgence upon this basis must result in a loss of vital energy which will deprive the man of the strength and vigor needed for the performance of his life takes * * *
 - B –Macfadden
